jQuery(document).ready(function(){
  // icon mail after youtube
  jQuery('.td-header-sp-top-menu').find('.td-social-icon-wrap:nth-of-type(3)').clone().appendTo('.td-header-sp-top-menu')

  // create elements for sticky menu
  jQuery("#td-top-mobile-toggle").append( "<span class='mobile-search-icon-btn'><i class='td-icon-search'></i></span>" );
  jQuery(".td-header-sp-recs form.td-search-form .td-head-form-search-wrap").append('<button id="close"><i class="fa fa-times" aria-hidden="true"></i></button>');
  // fix for logo
  var sticky = 'https://www.prad.de/wp-content/themes/prad-theme/images/logo-mobile_sticky.png';
  jQuery("div.td-container").find('.td-mobile-logo').append('<img style="display:none" class="sticky-img" src='+sticky+'>');

  // show/hide sticky search
  jQuery(".mobile-search-icon-btn").click(function(){
    jQuery(".td-header-sp-recs form.clone-element").addClass('show-search')
  });


    // fix for avatar link and clone elements
    var newLink = '/wp-admin';
    var elementToClone = jQuery('.td-header-sp-recs').clone();
    // debugger;
    elementToClonex = jQuery( elementToClone.html() );
    elementToClonex.find('#td-header-search').attr('id','td-header-search-clone');
    elementToClonex.find('#td-header-search-top').attr('id','td-header-search-top-clone');
    elementToClonex.find('#close').attr('id','close-clone');
    elementToClonex.appendTo('div.td-header-menu-wrap').addClass('clone-element').attr('tabindex', '-1').removeClass('td-search-form');
    jQuery('#close-clone').click(function(event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        jQuery(this).closest('form').addClass('hidden');
        // jQuery('.mobile-search-icon-btn').trigger('click');
    });


    jQuery('.td-container .td-header-sp-top-widget').find('ul.top-header-menu').clone().appendTo('div.td-header-menu-wrap').addClass('clone-avatar');
    jQuery('div.td-header-menu-wrap').find('ul.clone-avatar li a').removeClass('td-login-modal-js').attr('href', newLink);
    if (jQuery('.top-header-menu').is('.td_ul_logout')) {
      jQuery('ul.clone-avatar').find('img.avatar').attr('src', 'https://www.prad.de/wp-content/themes/prad-theme/images/icons/user_green.svg').attr('width', 25).attr('height', 25).removeAttr('srcset');
    }

    jQuery(".td-container .td-header-menu-wrap button#close").click(function(event){
      event.preventDefault();
      jQuery(".td-container div.td-header-menu-wrap form.clone-element").removeClass('show-search');
    });


  if(jQuery(window).width() > 768 ) {
    jQuery(window).scroll(function(){
         if ( jQuery(window).scrollTop() > 150 && jQuery('.td-header-menu-wrap').is('.td-affix')){
             jQuery('div.td-container .td-header-sp-logo').find('.td-main-logo').hide();
         }
         else {
           jQuery('div.td-container .td-header-sp-logo').find('.td-main-logo').show();
         }

    });
  }

});
</script>

<div class="td-module-meta-info">
<span class="author"><span class="td-post-author-name td-block vcard post-author"><a class="url fn" data-wpel-link="internal" href="https://www.prad.de/author/simon-blohm/" rel="noopener noreferrer">Simon Blohm</a> <span>-</span> </span></span> <span class="td-post-date"><time class="entry-date updated td-module-date test1" datetime="2018-10-31T22:15:06+00:00">31.10.2018</time></span> <div class="td-module-comments"><a data-wpel-link="internal" href="https://www.prad.de/buisness/eizo-business-area/#respond" rel="noopener noreferrer">0</a></div> </div>
<div class="td-excerpt">
                Qualität kann man sehen – zumindest, wenn man vor einem EIZO High-End-Monitor sitzt, denn zur Herstellung der werden nur beste Komponenten verwendet. Durch die hohe Material- und Fertigungsqualität und lange Garantiezeit von bis zu fünf Jahren profitiert jeder Anwender. (Werbung)            </div>

<div class="td-module-meta-info">
<span class="author"><span class="td-post-author-name td-block vcard post-author"><a class="url fn" data-wpel-link="internal" href="https://www.prad.de/author/andreas-roth/" rel="noopener noreferrer">Andreas Roth</a> <span>-</span> </span></span> <span class="td-post-date"><time class="entry-date updated td-module-date test1" datetime="2017-11-01T14:47:55+00:00">01.11.2017</time></span> <div class="td-module-comments"><a data-wpel-link="internal" href="https://www.prad.de/buisness/lg-business-area/#respond" rel="noopener noreferrer">0</a></div> </div>

                Für Ihren Arbeits-PC sind LG Business Monitore die perfekte Wahl. Mit innovativen Features sorgen sie für optimale Arbeitsbedingungen. Ganz gleich ob im Großraumbüro oder im Home-Office und egal ob im 21:9 UltraWide Format, mit 4K-Auflösung oder als Zero Client Lösung. (Werbung)            </div>

<div class="item-details">
<h3 class="entry-title td-module-title"><a data-wpel-link="internal" href="https://www.prad.de/test-kaufberatung/produktsuche/fernseher-suche-datenbank/" rel="bookmark noopener noreferrer" title="Fernseher Suche">Fernseher Suche</a></h3>
<div class="td-module-meta-info">
<span class="author"><span class="td-post-author-name td-block vcard post-author"><a class="url fn" data-wpel-link="internal" href="https://www.prad.de/author/andreas-roth/" rel="noopener noreferrer">Andreas Roth</a> <span>-</span> </span></span> <span class="td-post-date"><time class="entry-date updated td-module-date test1" datetime="2016-04-21T14:33:27+00:00">21.04.2016</time></span> <div class="td-module-comments"><a data-wpel-link="internal" href="https://www.prad.de/test-kaufberatung/produktsuche/fernseher-suche-datenbank/#respond" rel="noopener noreferrer">0</a></div> </div>
<div class="td-excerpt">
                    Sie suchen ein TV-Gerät mit einer bestimmten Ausstattung? Mit Hilfe unserer Datenbank selektieren Sie genau die Modelle, die Ihrer Vorstellung entsprechen.                </div>
</div>

var renderInvisibleReCaptcha = function() {

    for (var i = 0; i < document.forms.length; ++i) {
        var form = document.forms[i];
        var holder = form.querySelector('.inv-recaptcha-holder');

        if (null === holder) continue;
		holder.innerHTML = '';

         (function(frm){
			var cf7SubmitElm = frm.querySelector('.wpcf7-submit');
            var holderId = grecaptcha.render(holder,{
                'sitekey': '6LfUbXwUAAAAAK7YHLpa3dYQXdAzzJ9KeejQ86SS', 'size': 'invisible', 'badge' : 'inline',
                'callback' : function (recaptchaToken) {
					if((null !== cf7SubmitElm) && (typeof jQuery != 'undefined')){jQuery(frm).submit();grecaptcha.reset(holderId);return;}
					 HTMLFormElement.prototype.submit.call(frm);
                },
                'expired-callback' : function(){grecaptcha.reset(holderId);}
            });

			if(null !== cf7SubmitElm && (typeof jQuery != 'undefined') ){
				jQuery(cf7SubmitElm).off('click').on('click', function(clickEvt){
					clickEvt.preventDefault();
					grecaptcha.execute(holderId);
				});
			}
			else
			{
				frm.onsubmit = function (evt){evt.preventDefault();grecaptcha.execute(holderId);};
			}


        })(form);
    }
};
</script>
